# ▣ THE REGISTER — the corpus's open questions, opened r3009

***`PROTECTED_OPEN` closed at r3001 with fifteen rows struck.*** **The live set is not fixed here and is not to be read from this line: `THE_FRONTIER` is generated from the rows below and is the authority on which are open.** *A masthead that names its own live rows goes stale the first time one is struck — this one named `PO-13`, `PO-14` and `PO-15` and two of those three were struck in this same file.*

**⚙ THE RULES, CARRIED FORWARD UNCHANGED:** *a row is struck when **its OBJECT is answered and the answer is receipted** — not when it is convenient, and never on a person-gate. **Read the row's own history, then the closures, then the receipts** (§⑤·8). **Calibrate a detector against known instances before believing a zero** (§⑤·6). **A closure's consequence does not have to name a row to reach it** (§⑤·7). **An audit must normalise the source the way the extraction did** (§⑤·9).*

⚠ ***AND THE LESSON THAT COST A HUNDRED TURNS: for eighty revisions the answer to "has this been done?" was usually YES, and the register did not know it. **Look before declaring a build.** These have been checked against that — but check again.***

## ⛭⛭ THE ROOMS — *which rows share a wall, and where each room is open (`r6443`)*

***The completeness test below asks whether every family has a row. This asks a different question of the
same nine: which rows discharge TOGETHER.*** *A row is a hole in a wall; a room closes when its walls meet.
**Grouped by shared discharge, not by sector** --- the sector headings in `THE_FRONTIER` are the generated
cut and are not this one.*

| room | rows | where it is open |
|---|---|---|
| **① the counterterm ledger** | `PO-23`, `PO-43` | **one**: the interacting tower's ultraviolet definition --- *`P07`'s last of three, and **not reachable by refining the free-spectrum method***. ⌗ *The entropy wall is gone at `r6471`: the ledger does not count a counterterm coefficient, so the branch is costless to it and was never a wall of this room* |
| **② the matter content, and the head** | `PO-30`, `PO-31` *(with struck `PO-26`, `PO-40`, `PO-41`)* | **one, with a direction** *(`r6479`)*: a generative law for the **content**. ***`PO-31`'s opening is the shadow of it*** --- every lap after the first inherits its cut's shape and **the head inherits nothing**, so the law that fixes a cut absent a progenitor is what would say what matter is on the head's collapse leg. **`PO-30` discharged discharges both; `PO-31` alone moves neither** |

| **③ the acoustic front and the interior** | `PO-13`, `PO-24`, `PO-25` | **one** *(was two; `60`'s `r6522` ran the joint fit)*: `PO-25`'s condition, $m(r)=o(1/r)$ at the origin. ⚠ *And `r6525` finds it is **not a reading**: $m(r)$ near the origin is set by the matter, and `P08` supplies the dynamics **given content** while not supplying the constitutive relation. **It awaits an interior evolution with chosen matter --- the same thing `PO-31`'s remainder awaits, in its own words "a modelling task awaiting a progenitor interior".*** ⇒ **`PO-30` wants a LAW; these two want a MODEL, so they are not downstream of it --- and `PO-30` stays the only one of the three answerable without first choosing something arbitrary** \rcpt{P03_the_last_two_openings_await_the_same_interior} |
| **④ --- ** | `PO-36` | **one**, and *it does not discriminate this framework* --- see below |

**⛭ ① IS THE CLOSEST TO CLOSING, AND ONE OF ITS WALLS WAS FOUND ONLY AT `r6437`.** *Both rows now carry
values --- the log on the degenerate combination, the Weyl-squared coefficient off it. `PO-43`'s two
replacement questions resolved to one answered and one **not independent**: a topological term contributes
no field equation, so its coefficient's only home is the horizon entropy. ⇒ ***That wall is shared with
`PO-32`, which is STRUCK*** --- so a room of the quantum sector is bounded in part by horizon
thermodynamics, and **nothing in the corpus carried that before `r6437`.** ⌗ *And the entropy side is no
longer merely open: the no-free-constants claim has a stake in the branch, so it is a **prediction**
rather than an opening.*

**⌗ ② WAS DRAWN AS TWO ROOMS UNTIL `r6479`.** *`PO-30` wants a **law** --- why a cut bends as it does --- and `PO-41`'s strike says what is owed at the head is **the matter on its collapse leg, and how many**. ***Every lap after the first inherits its cut's shape from its progenitor; the head, by construction, inherits nothing*** --- so what fixes the head's cut is exactly a law that picks a cut absent a progenitor. ⇒ **`PO-30` discharged would discharge what the head owes; the converse fails, a fact about one cut yielding no law.** So the opening has a **direction**, and `PO-31`'s is the shadow of `PO-30`'s. ⌗ *The corpus draws this nowhere --- "generative law" never occurs within reach of "head", "genealogy" or "progenitor" in the register or any paper, the two rows having sat in different sectors since they were opened.* ⚠ *Held at weight: a **structural reading**, not a computation. **It fails if `PO-30`'s law fixes only the cut-content RELATION and not which cut**, and that is where to attack it.* \rcpt{P08_the_content_law_and_the_head_are_one_wall_seen_twice}

**⌗ ②'s HEAD HALF IS MOSTLY CLOSED ALREADY, and by rows that are struck.** *$\eta$, $A_s$ and $n_s$ all terminate at
the head --- `PO-41` for the first, `r6427` for the others once the stationary-recursion route was
excluded. ***One question asked of three quantities***, and the corpus already names what is owed there:
the matter on the head's collapse leg.*

**⌗ ③ HAS THE MOST OPENINGS AND ONE INSTRUMENT.** *`PO-24`'s residual is weighed on `PO-13`'s two-arm
machinery; `PO-25` hands *what stands in the horizon's place* to `PO-13`'s dynamical interior. **Two of the
three openings are runs rather than readings**, which is why this room moves slowest from a chat.*

**⛔ AND ④ IS NOT A ROOM.** *`PO-36` shares a wall with nothing. `r6407` established that both frameworks
take the same $m(r)$ --- whatever gravitates is the bend --- so the measurement **discriminates the dark
fraction and not this construction**, and it discharges by an observation nobody has made rather than by
corpus work. ⌗ ***It is carried here because it is honestly open, not because it bounds anything***, and a
later pass may find it belongs off this board.*

⚠ **THIS IS A JUDGEMENT, RECORDED SO ITS CHANGE IS VISIBLE, and it is not generated.** *An earlier sketch
of it was made from memory and was wrong within two turns of being said --- three of its rows moved. **Read
it against the rows, not instead of them.***

## ⛭ THE COMPLETENESS TEST — *`r3969`, and it found a gap, so it is worth keeping*

***The ledger and this register are two different populations, and neither is a superset of the other.***
*`corpus/open_ledger.txt` holds every qualification SENTENCE in the papers, all 96 now read.
`THE_OPEN_PROBLEMS_LEDGER`'s FAMILIES are a coarser grain over the same physics. **A row can be homed
correctly and a family still have no row**, which is exactly what happened.*

| family | live row |
|---|---|
| 1 · matter branch-point crossing | ⛭ **`PO-30`** — *the crossing is well posed; what remains is the generative law* |
| ~~2~~ | *killed `r1280`* |
| 3 · inherited datum, $\eta$ and the onset | ⛭ **`PO-31`** — ***opened `r3969` by this test; `PO-16`'s own text had named it since `r3012` and no row carried it*** |
| 4 · irreducible interior reassignments | **`PO-25`** — *charged half; Kerr half closed `r2378`* |
| 5 · scalar-perturbation sector | **`PO-13`** + **`PO-24`** |
| 6 · propagating fermion sector | **`PO-26`** |
| 7 · world-correspondence | **`PO-26`** *(`PO-27` struck `r4245`; its open half folded here)* |
| 8 · interacting quantum tower | **`PO-23`** |

⌗ ***Three live rows are finer than the family grain and belong to no family***: *`PO-29` (the $-M/r^{3}$
attribution), `PO-32` (horizon thermodynamics with no realised horizon, reopened `r4317`), `PO-36` (which mass
the Hubble--Eddington radius tracks), together with `PO-30`'s generative-law half. That is not a defect — it
is the register resolving what the families bundle.*

⇒ ***So the test to run before calling this complete is not "is every row homed" but "does every family
have a row, and does every row have a family or a stated reason it is finer."***

A finite computation of the same kind as `PO-23`'s, on a different quantity, and nobody knows whether the answer is zero.* &#9670; ***A NEGATIVE REPORTED BECAUSE IT IS THE FIRST THING A READER WILL REACH FOR:*** *no collision with the ghost-free result. A Weyl-squared term carries a massive spin-2 ghost, but `P07` scopes its claim in the same sentence that states it — the **classical** nonlinear regime is ghost-free to all orders — and a quantum counterterm does not reach a classical statement. **The claim needs no scoping it does not already have.*** &#9670; ***AND A SECOND POSSIBLE ENTRY, CHIRALITY-TIED, NOT WORKED:*** *`P10` notes the Pontryagin density is non-zero at second order for a circularly polarised mode and reverses with handedness, a linearly polarised mode returning zero — **and that the zero is a property of the mode chosen and not of the geometry**. Whether a parity-odd entry is owed therefore depends on the tower's polarisation, on which the corpus has a position in the matter sector. **Nobody has joined those two.*** &#9670; ***THE PARITY-ODD ENTRY IS NOT OWED ON PRESENT GROUNDS, `r4545` — AND THE TEMPTING CHAIN FAILS AT TWO SEPARATE JOINS.*** *The five faces give a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ exchanging the helicities and grading fermion chirality. **A map that exchanges two things is a grading, and every parity-even theory has one.** The corpus's own criterion is not that a reflection exists but that **no connected action completes it** — stated at `sec:derived` and applied to the radiating sector, never to this tower.* &#9670; ***AND IT FAILS INDEPENDENTLY AT THE COUNTERTERM ITSELF.*** *A counterterm answers to a parity-odd divergence of the effective action, a property of the theory and the state, **not of a basis mode**; the two helicities span the same space in either basis and cancel on a parity-symmetric state. **So `P10`'s caveat cuts against the chain rather than for it** — a mode-dependent quantity cannot fix the counterterm in either direction, which bounds the caveat's own instrument rather than deferring the question to a polarisation.* &#9670; ***AND EVEN IF OWED IT IS NOT THE SAME KIND OF THING AS THIS ROW'S ENTRY.*** *The density is a total derivative and contributes no field equation, so whether it is a ledger entry at all is a question about **whether the audit counts topological terms** — which has never arisen because this is the first candidate. The Weyl-squared entry is a genuine coupling with an uncosted coefficient. **Two potential entries, different in kind, and only one is a constant in the sense the audit means.*** &#9670; ***QUESTION (i) IS ANSWERED, `r4547`, AND THE ANSWER IS NO.*** *The closed layer is a group manifold, so its connected isometry group is a quotient of $SU(2)\times SU(2)$; the transverse-traceless harmonics at level $k$ sit in $(\tfrac{k+1}{2},\tfrac{k-3}{2})$ and its swap, **whose dimensions reproduce the tower's own degeneracy exactly at every level from the floor upward** — checked here, ten at the floor as a self-dual and an anti-self-dual five. **The two labels differ by two and never coincide.** A connected group acts within an isotypic component and cannot carry a representation to an inequivalent one, so the exchange is the outer automorphism and lies on the disconnected component. **The criterion is met in the strongest available form: the handedness is an irrep label.*** &#9670; ***SO THE TOWER IS CHIRALLY CAPABLE — WHICH IS THE RESULT, AND IT IS NOT THE ACHIRALITY BRANCH.*** *It is a second place where geometric chirality can live, and the sharper of the two: in the radiating sector the handedness is the sign of a conserved quantity, here it is a representation label. **And the join to the wall is sharper than both being chiral** — the index obstruction renders a connected-gauge fermion spectrum vector-like, and the tower is chiral in exactly the $SU(2)_L\times SU(2)_R$ sense that obstruction works in. Same group, same decomposition, opposite outcomes, one stated reason.* &#9670; ***AND THE PARITY-ODD ENTRY IS STILL NOT OWED — but the first of the two failures is removed, so the second now carries the whole verdict alone.*** *A counterterm answers to the effective action, and the effective action answers to **the state** rather than to the tower's capacity. **The marked step, and `63` marks it as one rather than assuming it:** the boundary condition is Hartle–Hawking regularity imposed fibre by fibre, and **nothing in its statement refers to helicity** — so the two towers are populated alike and the parity-odd content cancels between them. `63` has not run that check.* &#9670; ***AND IT REACHES THE LEDGER CLAIM NOT AT ALL.*** *The scoping stands as landed at eight sites; the Weyl-squared entry is parity-even and stands uncosted; the computed constant stands. **No rescue is offered and none is available here.*** &#9670; ***SO TWO QUESTIONS REPLACE IT, INDEPENDENT OF EACH OTHER AND NEITHER WORKED:*** *(i) does a connected isometry of the closed layer identify the two helicities of an $S^3$ tensor harmonic — a finite representation-theoretic question on the corpus's own criterion; and (ii) **does the ledger count topological terms**, which is a scope question about the audit rather than a physics question about the tower.* &#9670; ***NOT CLAIMED:*** *that the tower is achiral. What is claimed is that the argument for its being chiral needs a step the corpus does not have — and that the corpus asserts gravitational chirality for the radiating sector on a criterion it has not applied here, which is a different thing and not evidence for the first.* &#9670; ***QUESTION (ii) IS ANSWERED r6849 (66), FROM `P17`'s OWN TEXT, AND THE ANSWER IS THAT THE AUDIT DOES NOT RANGE OVER IT AND SHOULD NOT.*** *§ledger read whole partitions the constants two ways and says what each is: the real-geometric gauges $c,\Lambda,G$, each **a nameable feature of the substrate and its cuts**; and the thermal $\hbar,k_B$, standard horizon thermodynamics whose CR-specific content is **the closing of the one quantum freedom**, named there as the self-adjoint extension. **A topological term's finite part is neither**: it names no feature of the substrate, since a topological term contributes no field equation, and it is not the extension.* &#9670; ***AND THE AUDIT'S SUBJECT IS WHAT MAKES THIS SCOPING CORRECT RATHER THAN CONVENIENT.*** *The ledger counts what the construction must supply **to fix the geometry and close its one quantum freedom**. A coefficient whose only observable is a constant entropy shift enters no geometric prediction and no extension, so counting it would be counting a different kind of thing under the same name --- **which is the defect `r6437` made and `r6471` retracted, in the other direction.*** &#9670; ***SO THE ROW'S REMAINING CONTENT IS ONE UNRUN CHECK AND ONE DECLINED QUESTION, AND NEITHER IS THE ROW'S TITLE.*** *The check: whether Hartle--Hawking regularity, imposed fibre by fibre, populates the two helicity towers alike --- **nothing in its statement refers to helicity**, and if that holds the parity-odd content cancels between them and the entry is not owed at all. The declined question is whether $S=A/4$ carries to a cosmological horizon, which `P17` declines in terms and says would be a result rather than a gap were it to fail. **The row cannot close above the check**, and the check is routed.* &#9670; ***A CANDIDATE NAMED AND DELIBERATELY NOT BUILT:*** *a log needs a subtraction point, the corpus holds there is no second physical length, and in a discrete mode sum the subtraction point is a mode number — of which this tower has a canonical one, its own floor. **Whether that fixes the constant or merely names a convention is a question, not a result**, and if it is checked the burden is to show the floor is forced rather than convenient.* |
**⛭⛭ STANDING ORDER r6861 (Daryl): A ROW'S REMAINDER EARNS A ROW.** *When a row is struck, whatever it leaves live --- an edge it moved, a question it closed above, a candidate it named and did not build --- **is opened as its own row in the same pass**, with what is already ruled out carried across so the new row starts where the old one ended. A remainder recorded only in the struck row's text, in a paper's prose or in the changelog is buried: those are records, and **a node works from the frontier**. The five rows below were opened on this order after nine rows became two when they should have been seven --- a smaller seven, with a more consolidated corpus propping them up to be attacked the same way.*

| ~~**PO-47**~~ | **WHAT DISPLACES THE FOURTH ACOUSTIC PEAK.** &#9670; ***STRUCK r6877 (66, on the code seat's r6875+32): THE STOPPING RULE FIRED --- THE SKY CANNOT TELL THE TWO MODELS APART THERE.*** *Located through one procedure with the likelihood's own bandpower covariance propagated, this arm sits **$0.9$ to $1.3$ standard deviations** from the measured fourth peak across the admissible window. **Not called comfortably inside**: the reading runs across that range, so what is established is \emph{not resolved} rather than \emph{zero}, and both numbers are carried. The three candidates were not run.* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THE SEAT CORRECTED ITS OWN YARDSTICK FIRST, WHICH IS THE POINT.*** *Its earlier spread was a \emph{procedure} spread --- how far the answer moves as the fitting window is swept on one realisation --- **and nobody had propagated the sky's statistical uncertainty at all.** Through the same fit the combined spread is more than twice what was being used: **the half it had been quoting was the smaller half.*** &#9670; ***AND THE ROW'S OWN $+10.1$ WAS NEVER THIS NUMBER.*** *It was the paper's stored quartet against the paper's stored sky --- two different procedures. Through one locator the offset is $+4.65$, and **most of it is shared with the control**, which sits high of the located sky by $1.2$ standard deviations against this arm's $2.1$. A shared offset, named and not pursued.* &#9670; ***AND AN INSTRUMENT TRAP REPORTED AS A FINDING AND KEPT AS A GATE.*** *The earlier locator finds peaks by free extremum search --- right on a smooth spectrum, **unusable on a noisy one**, latching onto noise maxima seventy multipoles away under the covariance. The window was chosen on the bias-variance trade and not for the answer, with both rejected widths given, and a control plants known displacements and recovers them, **so the null is not the machinery failing to see.*** &#9670; **THE ROW AS IT STOOD:** &#9670; ***OPENED r6861 (66) AS `PO-13`'s REMAINDER --- the row it was struck from answered its own question and left this.*** *With the handover at the branch point and the background fixed by the distance data alone, the comb comes out at $298.0$ against the sky's $298.4$ and the first three peaks land within a grid step; **the fourth is about one per cent high**, and the offsets keep their sign and order across all four. **Reproduced on a second instrument** at $1132$ against $1134$, so it belongs to the spectrum rather than to either implementation.* &#9670; ***WHAT IS ALREADY RULED OUT.*** *The phase intercept is not a residual --- through one locator the arm sits inside the sky's own locating spread, and the offset's sign flips between the three-peak and four-peak fits. The damping envelope is excluded: imposing the control's moves the peak a seventh of a multipole against an offset of two, and the inverse operation returns the same displacement with the opposite sign. The driving is the control's to four per cent. A parameter refit does not move it.* &#9670; **WHAT WOULD DISCHARGE IT:** *a mechanism that displaces the fourth peak and not the first three, or a demonstration that at the spreads the locating carries there is nothing to displace.* | P15 `sec:refit-bound`, `sec:scope` |
| ~~**PO-48**~~ | **DOES $S=A/4$ CARRY TO A COSMOLOGICAL HORIZON ON THIS READING?** &#9670; ***STRUCK r6869 (66, on node 60's r6864): THE QUESTION HAS ONE ANSWER PER MEMBER, AND THE TWO HORIZONS THIS CORPUS USES FALL ON OPPOSITE SIDES --- WHICH IS WHY IT LOOKED UNDECIDABLE.*** *At the substrate's own horizon the Noether-charge construction has its hypotheses and **something varies that reaches the horizon term** at order one, so the law carries there; the "empty first law" route the order floated is not the answer. **At the forced member it cannot**: the double root makes the surface gravity vanish identically --- falling monotonically to zero along the family --- and at vanishing surface gravity the first law admits **no solution** for the entropy variation. \emph{Undetermined rather than false}, which is the sharper statement.* &#9670; ***AND `P17`'s DECLINATION IS RIGHT ABOUT ONE HORIZON AND WRONG ABOUT THE OTHER.*** *§ledger's horizon is the substrate's, where the law carries; the cosmology's branch point rides the forced member, where it cannot. **The question had been asked of two objects at once**, and the paper now settles it per member.* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THE STATED REASON DOES NOT SURVIVE RESTORING THE UNITS.*** *"The temperature is built from $\alpha$ alone" holds only where the thermal gauges are set to one --- with units restored it is cross-register too. **The real asymmetry is that the entropy is dimensionless and the temperature is not**, which bears on which quantity the framework may quote and not on whether either holds, and **cuts the opposite way from a defect**. `P17` carries the corrected reason.* &#9670; ***SO THE TWO CLOSURES RESTING ON THIS ROW DISCHARGE EITHER WAY***, *as §ledger said they would: both branches are results. **And nothing was imported** --- the value is never assumed, only the existence of a first law is tested.* &#9670; ⚠ ***ONE HINGE NAMED AND LEFT TO THE GATE, AND IT IS A READING OF INTENT RATHER THAN A COMPUTATION:*** *whether the offset family is a family of \emph{solutions} or of \emph{sections of one solution}. §ledger's wording points to the second, **which would void the variation at the substrate horizon too** --- so it is carried as `PO-53` rather than decided here.* &#9670; **THE ROW AS IT STOOD:** &#9670; ***OPENED r6861 (66) --- and it is overdue: `P17` declines it in terms, and TWO struck rows close above it.*** *`PO-24` and `PO-43` both terminate at this question without it being carried anywhere, which is a question propping two closures while belonging to no row.* &#9670; ***WHY IT IS NOT A LOOSE END BUT A REAL FORK.*** *`P17` §ledger takes the temperature and never the entropy, and says why: $T$ is built from $\alpha$ alone --- one register --- while the entropy is a ratio of $\alpha$ to $\ell_P$ and is **a count taken across the register split**. So whether the area law carries here is a question about that split and not an import from black-hole thermodynamics.* &#9670; ***AND `P17` HAS ALREADY SAID WHAT FAILURE WOULD MEAN:*** *"were it to fail to carry, that would be a result and not a gap" --- **so both outcomes are results and neither is a debt.** If it carries, a quantum-register constant becomes visible in the one cross-register quantity; if it does not, the Gauss--Bonnet coefficient has no home at all.* | P17 `sec:ledger`; P10 |
| ~~**PO-49**~~ | **THE CHARGED INTERIOR'S THIRD REGIME.** &#9670; ***OPENED r6861 (66) AS `PO-25`'s REMAINDER.*** *That row is struck on its own question --- the obstruction is destroyed on both charge readings and was never total across the shells --- and the verification reported a turning-point structure with **three regimes where the reading stated two**, the third being over-extremality. **Nothing carries it.*** &#9670; **WHAT WOULD DISCHARGE IT:** *what the lap does in that regime, on the same exact interior, and whether it is reachable on a progenitor of the mass this cosmology requires --- the struck row's own scoring putting the charge-to-mass threshold near $10^{-2}$, so reachability is a question about astrophysical charge and not about the geometry.* &#9670; ⛔ ***STRUCK r6913 (66, on node 60's r6912): THE THIRD REGIME IS NOT A REGIME.*** *With $\beta=B\sin^{4}\chi-Q^{2}$ the third case is the one where the turning-point quadratic has **no real root**, and its leading coefficient is $-\sin^{2}\chi<0$ --- so no real root means it never changes sign and $(\dd R/\dd\tau)^{2}<0$ at **every** radius, verified over sixteen decades. ⇒ ***No shell exists with that data at any radius, so the lap does nothing there because there is nothing to do***: the third case is the boundary of **admissible initial data**, $Q^{2}\le(B+A^{2}/4)\sin^{4}\chi$, and not a third behaviour of the motion. **And its indicator contains no dynamical variable**, so no shell crosses the boundary during the lap either, which closes the dynamical reading of reachability as well as the parametric one.\rcpt{P03_the_third_regime_is_the_boundary_of_admissible_data_and_not_a_behaviour_of_the_lap} &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THE ORDER'S HINT WAS WRONG, WHICH IS THIS SEAT'S TO OWN.*** *It put the threshold near $10^{-2}$ and concluded reachability was a question about astrophysical charge. **The $10^{-2}$ is the OBSTRUCTION's threshold --- the second regime's boundary --- and this regime's is $1.000134$, over-extremality**, because $2a_{\rm eq}/M=2.67\times10^{-4}$ makes the matter terms negligible against $M^{2}$. ⇒ *So reachability is settled with no bound on astrophysical charge at all, and the answer to which of the two the row was asking is: **the geometry, for both halves**.* &#9670; ***NO REMAINDER, AND ONE CLAUSE LANDED.*** *`P3` \S`charged` names the charged closed loop as an extension it does not build and said nothing about what that extension admits. **It now carries the bound** --- no shell beyond the threshold, the boundary one of data and not of motion, and the threshold at over-extremality --- which is settled without building the extension.* | P3 `sec:charged` |
| **PO-50** | **THE TURNAROUND MEASUREMENT WITH TURNAROUND MASSES.** &#9670; ***OPENED r6861 (66) AS `PO-36`'s REMAINDER.*** *That row is struck: the radius tracks the dynamical mass, entailed rather than observed. **What its own measurement could not do is discriminate**, because the ensemble's ratios run $1.20$ to $2.33$ above the spherical bound at the dynamical mass, and the authors' leading explanation is the systematic the corpus had named --- **virial mass substituting for turnaround mass**.* &#9670; **WHAT WOULD DISCHARGE IT:** *an ensemble with masses measured inside the turnaround rather than at the virial radius, with the radius still independent of internal dynamics. **The bound-to-attained factor is parameter-free** and the guard is four-way, so the instrument for reading such a sample is built and only the sample is missing.* &#9670; ***THE SWEEP IS RUN r6893 (66), AND IT CORRECTS THIS ROW'S OWN PREMISE.*** *The row says the authors' leading explanation for the ensemble's excess is the systematic the corpus had named --- a virial mass standing in for a turnaround mass. **It is the right systematic and it is not large enough.** Because the bound goes as $M^{1/3}$, rescuing a ratio $R$ needs $M_{\rm ta}/M_{\rm vir}=R^{3}$: for the six groups that is $12.6$, $5.4$, $6.4$, $6.0$, $2.6$ and $1.7$, against a measured turnaround-to-virial mass ratio of $1.2$ to $2.2$ in the caustic ensembles. **So the substitution accounts for the two mildest rows and not the four largest** --- though none of the six is individually significant on its own error bar, which may be all there is to explain.* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THE TWO ENSEMBLES THAT LOOK USABLE ARE CIRCULAR --- MEASURED HERE, NOT READ OFF.*** *Both define their outer radius at a **fixed overdensity** rather than measuring it: at $\bar\rho=3.5\rho_c$ the ratio of that radius to the bound is $(\Omega_\Lambda/1.75)^{1/3}=0.737$, and at $\bar\rho=5.56\rho_c$ it is $0.632$ --- **pure numbers carrying no datum**, identical for every cluster in either sample. A test run on them cannot come out any way but the way it is defined to.* &#9670; ***WHAT THE LITERATURE DOES HOLD, AND WHERE THE ROW NOW SITS.*** *No published catalogue carries a kinematically determined turnaround radius **and** an enclosed mass at it; every candidate fails one side. The nearest thing in print is the original proposal's own run at the caustic-detection radius, which is data-limited rather than definitional and which reported several clusters above the spherical bound. **And the direction of the correction is itself contested**: the caustic route puts the turnaround-enclosed mass above the virial one, while the Local Volume flow route puts the mass inside the zero-velocity surface at about six tenths of it. ***That disagreement is the first thing to settle, because the row's arithmetic runs through it.*** One system --- Coma --- now has a kinematic turnaround radius, a caustic profile and wide-field lensing on the same object, so the single-object version is reachable; the **ensemble** is not, and assembling one is a reanalysis of public redshifts rather than a new survey.* &#9670; ⚠ ***AND THE CORPUS IS NOT EXPOSED HERE, WHICH IS WHY THIS SHARPENS THE ROW RATHER THAN THREATENING ANYTHING.*** *`P3` states the radius as general relativity's with $\Lambda$ and says it discriminates no cosmology, and the landed confrontation is the Local Group, where the mass is measured independently of the radius. **The ensemble was never load-bearing.*** | P4; P17 |
| ~~**PO-51**~~ | **THE LOG'S SUBTRACTION POINT, AND WHETHER THE TOWER'S FLOOR IS FORCED.** &#9670; ***STRUCK r6867 (66, on the code seat's r6863+31): THE ANSWER SPLITS, AND THE ROW'S PREMISE RAN TWO STATEMENTS TOGETHER.*** *The floor **is** forced as a mode: $m=3$ is the lowest transverse-traceless harmonic the three-sphere admits --- one of the representation labels vanishes exactly there and is negative below, where no representation exists, and the degeneracy is zero at $m=2$. The tower is gapped with no zero mode and no soft region, **so the infrared is regulated by the geometry**, confirmed against a control where a floor near zero blows an infrared-weighted sum by thirteen orders.* &#9670; ***AND THE LOG IS REAL SCHEME-INDEPENDENTLY.*** *The Dirichlet series has a simple pole at the origin with residue exactly the corpus's coefficient, so zeta regularisation does not evade the subtraction point.* &#9670; ***BUT THE SUBTRACTION POINT IS A CONVENTION, AND THE DECIDING TEST COMES OUT AGAINST IN TWO STEPS.*** *A mode number is **dimensionless**, so the log carries no length for any choice of floor and **the one-scale claim --- this row's whole motivation --- protects every choice equally and cannot single out the floor.** And `P10`'s own argument makes the counterterm basis one-dimensional on the admitted family, checked here on the tower's own background rather than a nearby one, so the log's counterterm is degenerate with the cosmological term and `P17` absorbs a constant vacuum energy into the one observed curvature: **a change of subtraction point moves a constant that is reabsorbed into the one measured gauge.*** &#9670; ***AND THE FLOOR FIXES THE CONSTANT TO NOTHING:*** *subtracting there leaves a number neither zero nor named. **So the answer is "convenient", and it is a result rather than a failure** --- the one-scale claim comes out stronger where it is true, a discrete sum needing no second \emph{length} for any subtraction point, and the corpus stops carrying a determination it was not entitled to.* &#9670; ***AND THE THIRD TEST'S VERDICT IS READ AT ITS WEIGHT RATHER THAN BANKED.*** *The coefficient is identical across floors while the finite part shifts by exactly the log of their ratio --- which comes out **for** the row, and **could only ever have settled the question against**: a constant nothing measures does not become determined by the coefficient above it being fixed.* &#9670; ***AND THE FENCE HELD.*** *Nothing uses the coupled tower and every number is the free spectrum's, so `PO-23` is not reached; where the shear breaks the counterterm degeneracy the Weyl-squared coefficient is a separate entry, named and not claimed. **The coefficient itself is not discharged.*** &#9670; **THE ROW AS IT STOOD:** &#9670; ***OPENED r6861 (66) AS `PO-43`'s NAMED-AND-UNBUILT CANDIDATE.*** *A log needs a subtraction point; the corpus holds there is no second physical length; and in a discrete mode sum the subtraction point is a mode number --- **of which this tower has a canonical one, its own floor**.* &#9670; ***AND THE BURDEN IS STATED AT THE OUTSET RATHER THAN DISCOVERED:*** *whether that fixes the constant or merely names a convention is a question and not a result, and **the burden is to show the floor is forced rather than convenient.** It was deliberately not built while `PO-43`'s check was outstanding; that check has come out for the claim, so the candidate is now the row's own.* | P10 `sec:lock`; P17 `sec:ledger` |
| ~~**PO-52**~~ | **WHERE THE SHEAR BREAKS THE COUNTERTERM DEGENERACY.** &#9670; ***OPENED r6867 (66) AS `PO-51`'s REMAINDER, ON THE STANDING ORDER.*** *That row closes because the log's counterterm is **degenerate with the cosmological term** on the admitted background family --- the degeneracy being conformal flatness, checked on the tower's own background --- so a change of subtraction point moves a constant reabsorbed into the one measured gauge. **The degeneracy is what does the work, and it is a property of that family.*** &#9670; ***SO THE QUESTION IS WHAT HAPPENS WHERE IT BREAKS.*** *At second order in the shear the family is no longer conformally flat, and there the Weyl-squared entry is a separate coefficient rather than an absorbed constant --- **which is `PO-43`'s uncosted entry, named there and named again here, and carried by neither.** The code seat named it rather than claiming it, which is why it earns a row instead of a sentence.* &#9670; **WHAT WOULD DISCHARGE IT:** *whether the admitted family reaches second order in the shear at all on this construction --- if it does not, the entry has no domain and that closes it; and if it does, whether the broken degeneracy leaves a coefficient anything observes, which is the same cross-register question `PO-48` reached and did not close.* &#9670; ***THE CHEAP HALF IS ANSWERED r6897 (66, on node 60's r6894), AND IT COMES OUT AGAINST CLOSING.*** *The question was whether the admitted family reaches second order in the shear at all --- if not, the Weyl-squared entry has no domain and the row closes on that alone. **It does reach there.** The Weyl invariant vanishes identically on the admitted family, which is the conformal flatness `PO-51` rests on and is used here as the calibration; on the construction's own on-shell Gowdy--de~Sitter confined wave it is zero at zeroth and first order in the shear amplitude and **non-vanishing at second**. ⇒ *So the entry has a domain and the row keeps its second half: whether the broken degeneracy leaves a coefficient anything observes.* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THE TWO ROWS DO NOT COLLAPSE, WHICH THE ORDER SAID WOULD BE WORTH AS MUCH.*** *This seat sent `PO-55` and `PO-52` together on the reading that an unobserved coefficient and an unobserved area difference might be one fact. **They are not.** `PO-55` closes by a quantity's **non-existence**; this row's quantity **exists** and the open question is whether anything sees it. *The method transfers --- ask whether it exists before asking whether it is seen --- and the result does not.** ⌗ *What the registers cannot see and what is simply not there are two different answers, and only one row gets the second one.* &#9670; ⛔ ***STRUCK r6909 (66, on node 60's r6898): IT CLOSES, AND NOT BY THE ROUTE THE ORDER OFFERED.*** *The order allowed one closure --- that nothing observes the admitted second-order shear configurations. **That is not what this finds.** The configurations are admitted and the non-vanishing invariant is real. **What fails is the step from a non-vanishing INVARIANT to a moved OBSERVABLE**, because conformal flatness was never the only degeneracy in play.* &#9670; ***IN FOUR DIMENSIONS THE REDUCTION IS AN IDENTITY, NOT A PROPERTY OF THE FAMILY.*** *$C^{2}=E_{4}+2(R_{\mu\nu}R^{\mu\nu}-R^{2}/3)$ holds on **any** metric, so no order in the shear can separate the curvature-squared invariants --- verified on closed FLRW with the scale factor free, where $C^{2}=0$ and both remainders are non-zero so the identity reads more than $0=0$, and on Schwarzschild--de~Sitter where $C^{2}=48M^{2}/r^{6}$ does not vanish. **And the Gauss--Bonnet half reaches no field equation**: the Lanczos identity holds ON the shear configuration, at the amplitudes where $C^{2}$ is non-vanishing, to $1.6\times10^{-30}$ at thirty digits. ⇒ *So a change of subtraction point moves the dynamics only through the Ricci-squared remainder, which the field equations trade for the stress tensor, and on the substrate $C^{2}=E_{4}-8\Lambda^{2}/3$ exactly.* ***That is `PO-51`'s conclusion reached from an identity rather than from a property a deformation could remove, so the shear cannot take it away*** --- and `r6894` is the premise of this result rather than a casualty of it\rcpt{P10_the_shear_breaks_conformal_flatness_but_not_the_gauss_bonnet_degeneracy_so_nothing_sees_the_coefficient}. &#9670; ***WHAT LANDED IN THE PAPER, AND IT IS LOAD-BEARING RATHER THAN DECORATIVE.*** *`P10` concluded the counterterm basis is one-dimensional **because the admitted background family is** --- which is exactly the premise second-order shear threatens. It now carries the identity, with the family supplying only the rest of the step, and states the one restriction: off shell $C^{2}$ is independent and nothing here bounds it.* &#9670; ⚠ ***AND AN INSTRUMENT FAILURE IS ON THE RECORD BECAUSE THE CALIBRATION CAUGHT IT.*** *The first Lanczos tensor raised one index of one Riemann factor and not the other, and returned $0.8$ on pure de~Sitter where the identity is exact --- **silent everywhere except on the case with a known answer**. The $\epsilon=0$ row is kept as that control, which is the same lesson two seats learned this week from tolerances.* &#9670; ***NO REMAINDER.*** *The Gauss--Bonnet integral does shift the on-shell action by a topology-fixed constant --- **but it does so at zero shear as much as at non-zero**, so the broken degeneracy is not what produces it and it is not what this row asked about.* | P10 `sec:lock`; P17 `sec:ledger`; and `PO-48` |
| ~~**PO-53**~~ | **IS THE OFFSET FAMILY A FAMILY OF SOLUTIONS, OR OF SECTIONS OF ONE SOLUTION?** &#9670; ***STRUCK r6871 (66, gating `main`): IT IS BOTH, AND THE TWO ARE NOT ALTERNATIVES --- WHICH IS WHY THE HINGE LOOKED LIKE A FORK.*** *`P08`'s kernel theorem settles the first half in terms: the vanishing of the stress in that gauge **is** a first-order linear equation **whose entire solution space is the one-parameter family**. So the members are solutions of the induced vacuum equation, and that is a theorem rather than a reading. And `P17` §ledger settles the second: they are sections of one substrate, the mass being the offset of the section from the central geodesic. **A construction whose point is that the vacuum sector is obtained by sectioning one object will say both, and neither statement is the other's denial.*** &#9670; The general level needs the gradient terms and is **not computed**; 60 asserts neither the general-level shift nor any consequence of it, and recomputes nothing downstream. **This row exists so that the flag is a live item rather than a sentence in a reply.*** &#9670; ⛭⛭ ***WHY IT IS A ROW AND NOT A NOTE: THE QUANTITY IT MOVES IS THE ONE `PO-23` IS ABOUT.*** *`P10` states the logarithmic coefficient as the $1/m$ term of $d(m)\mu(m)$ --- **an exact functional of the spectrum**, which is precisely what makes it immune to renormalisation of the frequencies and what the whole counterterm argument rests on. ⇒ *If the frequency is $m^{2}-1$ rather than $m^{2}-3$ at every level, that coefficient necessarily changes.* ⌗ **This seat’s own arithmetic, stated as unreceipted and to be receipted before anything is landed:** $2(m^{2}-4)\sqrt{m^{2}-1}$ expands as $2m^{3}-9m+(15/4)/m$ against the corpus’s $2m^{3}-11m+(39/4)/m$.* &#9670; ⛭ ***AND WHAT IS AT RISK IS A NUMBER AND A DESCRIPTION, NOT THE RESULT --- WHICH IS WHY THIS IS AN ORDINARY CORRECTION AND NOT A THREAT TO THE ROW IT TOUCHES.*** *The coefficient stays non-zero, so the logarithm is still unpartnered and still lands on a counterterm the ledger lacks. And the mass-shift argument keeps its FORM exactly: writing $\mu^{2}=m^{2}+u$ gives $1/m$ coefficient $-u(u+16)/4$, zero only at $u=0$ and $u=-16$, so **the one displacement that discharges the log is still exactly the offset that makes these frequencies non-integer** --- $+1$ where the paper says $+3$, and $+1$ is the net of the Laplacian’s $-3$ and the curvature term’s $+2$.* ⇒ **So the structure of `P10`’s central argument is robust under this and the figures in it are not.** &#9670; ⌷ ***WHAT ELSE WOULD MOVE, LISTED UNFILTERED RATHER THAN TRIAGED:*** *the $39/4$ and its two-regulator agreement at $9.749$; the $\delta=3$ discharge and the sentence naming it the curvature offset; the floor $\mu_n^{2}\ge6$ (which only strengthens); the quartic leader’s constant, which is the degeneracy and does **not** move, the harmonic count being untouched; and every receipt that pins any of these. **Nothing here is reclassified as harmless until it has been read.*** &#9670; **WHAT WOULD DISCHARGE IT:** *the reduction at general level with the gradient terms included, settling whether the curvature term contributes $+2$ at every level or only where the harmonics are frame-constant; and, if it does, every exact functional of the spectrum re-derived at the corrected frequency with its receipts re-pointed and its prose rewritten to one state.* | **OPEN — opened r6973.** *No prior state: the corpus has carried $\mu_n^{2}=n(n+2)-2$ as the frequency since the graviton lift was written, and nothing had asked whether the reduction puts the curvature term in it.* | `P10` §`lock`, the tower action and the logarithmic coefficient |

⚠ ***WHAT NEITHER TOOL CAN SEE, STATED BY THEM RATHER THAN DISCOVERED LATER:*** *ⓐ cannot see a needle built at run time, a regex pin, a haystack whose file is not named where it is assigned, or a distinctive number sitting alone in the wrong sentence --- **so it bounds the class from below, with no false positives, and not from above.** ⓑ cannot see a read made in a subprocess the receipt spawns, or one through a C extension.* ⌗ *Wiring ⓑ into CI is a suite run's cost and is this seat's call, deferred one revision: the two tools are new and their own seeding is one revision old.* &#9670; | ~~**PO-58**~~ | **THE LIFT'S NORMALIZABILITY THRESHOLD, ON THE OPERATOR THE CORPUS NOW USES.** &#9670; ⛭ ***OPENED r6921 (66) FROM THE SPIN-UP 69 READ, AS THE REMAINDER OF A FIX THAT LANDED ONLY HALF.*** *`P14`'s count passage rejected the growing zero-mode branch by a leaf-measure argument at the branch point: $\dd\ell\sim\sqrt{\lvert r\rvert/2M}\,\dd r$, so $\lvert r\rvert^{s}$ normalizes for $s>-\tfrac34$ and the growing $s=-\lambda$ would need $\lambda<\tfrac34$, which no $\lambda=j+\tfrac12$ attains. **On the operator the corpus now uses that argument is gone**: the read corrected the count passage to the current form, where near the branch point $f\to-2M/r$ makes the exponent $\propto i\sqrt{\lvert r\rvert}$, both chiralities a bounded phase and the measure integrable, so ***the crossing selects nothing and costs the norm nothing***\rcpt{W2_the_wall_zero_mode_on_the_operator_b60_fixes}.* &#9670; ⛔ ***BUT THE SAME $\tfrac34$ SURVIVES TWICE IN \S`lift`, AND THERE IT IS LOAD-BEARING.*** *The lift's mode content is a normalizability computation on **the lift's own measure at the three hinges** --- a different locus and a different measure from the branch point --- and it rejects the growing branch by "the condition $\lambda<\tfrac34$ that no $\lambda=j+\tfrac12$ attains". ⇒ *The second occurrence makes it structural rather than incidental: the deck's third case is excluded because it needs the three sectors decoupled, **which is the lift's $\lambda<\tfrac34$ rejection read as a condition on the hinges --- "one inequality deciding both conjuncts, in opposite senses".*** So a wrong threshold there moves two conclusions at once and in opposite directions.* &#9670; ⚠ ***WHAT IS AND IS NOT AT RISK, BECAUSE THE MARGIN IS WORTH STATING WITH THE DEFECT.*** *The smallest admissible $\lambda$ is $j=\tfrac12$, giving $\lambda=1$, so **any threshold below $1$ returns the same verdict** and the conclusion has room. ⛔ *What is at risk is the NUMBER and the derivation behind it, and the corpus does not get to quote a threshold whose argument it has replaced elsewhere in the same paper --- nor to assume the branch-point measure carries to the hinges, which is the step that has never been run.* &#9670; ⛔⛭ ***STRUCK r6929 (66, on node 60's r6926): THE LIFT SUPPLIES ITS OWN MEASURE AND ITS OWN EXPONENT, AND THEY RETURN THE SAME THRESHOLD BY DIFFERENT ARITHMETIC.*** *The row asked whether the branch-point measure carries to the hinges. ⇒ ***It does not have to.*** **$\ell=(2\alpha/3)\sin(3\theta/2)$ is REGULAR at a hinge** --- it vanishes at $0,120,240^{\circ}$ with $\dd\ell/\dd\theta\to\alpha$ there, and is extremal at the wall angles, both verified --- **which is the opposite of the branch point's and means the measure cannot supply the threshold at all.** *It comes from the EXPONENT, and the exponent is a cube root because the time map is three-to-one*: on the lift $\tilde\tau$ is purely imaginary, so $\sinh^{2}(i\phi)=-\sin^{2}\phi$ and $\lvert r\rvert^{3}\propto\sin^{2}(3\theta/2)$, giving $\lvert r\rvert\sim\theta^{2/3}$ at a hinge --- **the same three that makes a hinge crossing a half-loop in $\ell$ and multiplies the mode by $\omega^{\lambda}$.** So the growing branch integrates $\theta^{-4\lambda/3}$, convergent iff $\lambda<\tfrac34$, confirmed by explicit integration at $\tfrac12$ (finite), $\tfrac34$ and $1$ (divergent).* &#9670; ⌗ ***SO THE TWO DERIVATIONS ARE INDEPENDENT AND THE ORDER'S CONDITIONAL NEVER FIRES.*** *The branch point gives the threshold from $2s+\tfrac12>-1$ and the lift from $-4\lambda/3>-1$: **two loci, two measures, one number.** The threshold does not differ from $\tfrac34$, so both conjuncts of \S`lift`'s deck argument hold at the same value and the one inequality deciding both in opposite senses is undisturbed; with $\lambda=j+\tfrac12$ and $j$ half-integer, $\lambda\in\{1,2,3,\dots\}$, so **the margin is $1$ against $\tfrac34$** exactly as the row recorded it. ⇒ *And the defect the row opened on was real: the count passage's derivation WAS removed and WAS the only one the paper carried. `P14` now states the lift's own measure and exponent beside the threshold, so it no longer rests on an argument the same paper has replaced.*\rcpt{P14_the_lifts_own_measure_returns_the_same_three_quarters_so_the_removed_derivation_was_not_the_only_one} &#9670; ***NO REMAINDER.*** *The row asked one question, it is answered, and the margin it flagged as the thing not at risk was not at risk.* &#9670; **WHAT WOULD DISCHARGE IT:** *the hinge normalizability recomputed on the current operator and the lift's own measure, reporting the threshold it gives; and, if it differs from $\tfrac34$, whether both conjuncts of \S`lift`'s deck argument still hold at the new value, since they are said to turn on one inequality in opposite senses.* ⌗ *Not asked: a new operator, a new lift, or a re-derivation of the count, which \S`count` already carries in its corrected form.* | ~~**STRUCK r6929** — opened r6921.~~ *No prior state: the branch-point half was corrected by the read and this half was named by it and not worked.* | P14 `sec:lift` |
| **PO-56** | **THE ACOUSTIC CONTRAST DIFFERENCE: THE PROJECTION'S GEOMETRY, ON THE CORPUS'S TWO-RATE ASSIGNMENT.** &#9670; ***OPENED r6891 (66) AS `PO-13`'s LIVE REMAINDER, ON THE STANDING ORDER.*** *`PO-13` was struck at `r6790` because the handover at the branch point supplies the one-locus state the row asked for, and it routed what it left --- the acoustic phase and the fourth peak --- into `P15` \S`sec:scope`'s prose rather than into a row. **Both of those have since dissolved** --- the phase intercept sits inside the sky's own locating spread and the fourth peak is $0.9$ to $1.3$ standard deviations out with most of the offset shared with the control --- **and what replaced them is a different object that has never had a row.*** &#9670; ⌗ ***AND WHERE THE LEAD CAME FROM, RECORDED AT `r6963` BECAUSE IT WAS ABOUT TO BE LOST: DARYL READ IT OFF THE GRAPH AS AN AMPLITUDE DIFFERENCE, BEFORE ANY OF THIS WAS MEASURED.*** *That reading is what sent the code seat after this sector in the first place --- **not a statistic, not a $\chi^{2}$ decomposition, a look at the two spectra plotted together** --- and the eliminations then ran for six revisions before arriving where it started. ⇒ *`cc66.46` measured the excess as symmetric about the envelope, $1.067$ on the peak side against $1.056$ on the trough side and $5.7$ per cent at the maxima against $5.3$ at the minima: **an amplitude excess carried by peaks and troughs both, which is the graph's own reading with numbers under it**.* ⌗ *It is recorded here for the same reason the withdrawn hypotheses are: the row's history is where the next reader learns what kind of evidence moved it, and **a correct lead taken from a figure is a different kind of evidence from a measurement and is worth being able to tell apart.** ⚠ *It licenses nothing on its own --- the amplitude reading is carried by `cc66.46`'s decomposition and not by the figure --- and the four hypotheses this chat seat lost along the way were all statistical.* &#9670; ***WHAT THE OBJECT IS, MEASURED RATHER THAN NAMED.*** *With $\Delta$ the whitened difference of the two arms at their own minima, $\chi^{2}$ decomposes exactly as $282.96=177.88+27.82+77.26$, so the model difference carries three quarters of the excess and the rejection is not one sky's luck: this arm scores $1.43$ per bin against the control's $0.99$ on a typical sky. $\Delta$ is **trough-dominated** ($+0.011$ at the peaks against $-0.760$ at the troughs), its oscillatory part carries seven tenths of it, and that part is antisymmetric about the **envelope** rather than about each peak --- **a contrast, and not a phase and not an envelope**. This arm's acoustic oscillation is $1.040$ times the control's about its own envelope.* &#9670; ***WHAT IS ALREADY RULED OUT, CARRIED ACROSS SO THE ROW STARTS WHERE THE WORK ENDED.*** *The handover amplitude, **exactly** --- it is absorbed by the one fitted amplitude. The neutrino hierarchy's truncation depth ($93.7\%$ of $\lVert\Delta\rVert^{2}$ survives it), the early integrated Sachs--Wolfe term ($92.8\%$), and the continuity half of the driving ($87.6\%$). The Euler half reaches $68.3\%$ but at a direction cosine of $-0.16$, which is not the direction. And the whole amplitude--tilt--damping--position family together carries $5.73\%$, against $51\%$ for a single control-only direction that raises the oscillation at fixed envelope with nothing fitted.* &#9670; ***THE CHANNEL IS IDENTIFIED AND THE CAUSE IS NOT.*** *Removing the monopole and removing the Doppler term bracket the contrast direction with **opposite signs**, on the line-of-sight path and on the reporting path alike and with the same signs on both, so the reading is not an artefact of the path it first had to be measured on. **But neither deletion returns the two arms to a common contrast** ($1.0376$ and $1.0818$ against the base $1.0401$), so the bracket locates where the difference enters and not what produces it. About a third of $\lVert\Delta\rVert^{2}$ is unnamed.* &#9670; ⚑ ***THE ONE REACHABLE CANDIDATE HAS NOW BEEN POINTED AT THE QUESTION, AND IT IS RULED OUT --- `r6893`.*** *The free-streaming knob is located sub-bin by a parabola vertex and again by an envelope-normalised cross-correlation, on both arms and again at four times the multipole sampling.* ⚠ **The shift is NOT uniform, which CORRECTS `r6889`**: band by band it rises monotonically, $+2.9/+5.6/+9.4/+10.9/+12.6$ on the control against $+3.1/+5.7/+9.3/+10.9/+12.5$ on the arm --- *the uniformity `r6889` gated was the grid, and its tolerance was $0.05$ on integers that could only differ by a whole bin.* ⇒ ***AND IT IS COMMON TO THE TWO ARMS: the largest band-by-band difference is $0.17$ of a multipole and the extremum-averaged shift in units of each arm's own $\ell_A$ agrees to $1.7\times10^{-4}$*** --- so on this seat's own criterion, *a phase shift common to both is not a candidate for $\Delta$ and one that differs between them is*, **it is not a candidate**. ⌗ *And the drag half, kept apart from the phase and split in two because it is two things, says the same independently: removing free-streaming raises the **envelope** by $25.7\%$ and changes the peak-to-trough **contrast** by $-1.3\%$, on both arms, agreeing to $0.0004$ and $0.0006$ --- and $\Delta$ is a CONTRAST difference, so the knob's large effect is in the wrong quantity.* ⇒ *In $\Delta$'s own space the two arms' `NUFS` directions sit at a whitened cosine of $0.99918$; their difference freely rescaled could reach $8.7\%$ of $\lVert\Delta\rVert^{2}$ at a **negative** coefficient, and there is no such freedom, because both arms carry the same neutrino sector.* &#9670; ⚑⚑ ***AND THE HAZARD THIS ROW CARRIED IS DISCHARGED --- `r6893`, by an accounting and not by a fourth find.*** *Three switches had been found verified on a path they reach and used on a path they do not --- the tilt literal, the baryon density, and the two source terms --- and three by three routes is a **rate**, not a count.* ⇒ **The sweep enumerates all SIXTY environment switches the instrument reads, records from the source text which of the three source constructions and which of the two solver paths reads each, and measures fifty-five of them on both arms one switch at a time.** *Fifty-one have a live use site on the reporting path; the nine that do not come back **bit-identical** there --- all eighteen runs at exactly $0.0$ --- and again at full $\ell$ reach, where `DAMPX` and `RD` would have shown if they touched the damping tail.* ⇒ ***The set of bit-identical runs is EXACTLY the set four readings predict --- off-path, rate-identity on the control arm, the arm branch, and gated by another switch --- with no unexplained null and nothing explained away that in fact moved.*** &#9670; ⛭ ***TWO THINGS THE SWEEP DID TURN UP, neither of the `r6476` class and neither moving a reported number.*** *(i) `LRSFROM` moves the instrument's reported $r_s$ by a quarter, $145.38\to110.49$ Mpc, and its spectrum by **exactly zero**: $R_S$ and $\ell_A$ are **diagnostics**, reaching a `print` and the `SAVE` metadata and nothing else, and `hier_run` accepts $\ell_A$, $D_M$ and $r_s$ and uses none of the three --- so `r6476`'s reachability check for that switch was taken on the **header**, and the header is not the reported number.* ⇒ *And the consequence points the useful way: because no transfer function reads $\ell_A$, the printed $\ell_1/\ell_A=0.7312$ agreeing with the sky's $220.6/301.7$ is an agreement between **two independently computed quantities** and not a value fed in.* *(ii) `LATARG`, which the instrument calls the corpus's one fitted number, has **no root** at the arm's adjudicated background --- the onset solve raises across the whole widened bracket --- which is why the refit command supplies `ZSTART=3e7`, so nothing reported moves and the register had not said the switch is unreachable there.* &#9670; **WHAT WOULD DISCHARGE IT:** *a mechanism that raises this arm's acoustic oscillation about its own envelope by four per cent where the control's is not raised --- or a demonstration that the monopole-to-Doppler balance cannot differ between the arms at that size, which would move the search to the unnamed third.* &#9670; ⚠ ***AND TWO CONFIRMATIONS ARE OWED, RECORDED HERE BECAUSE THEY ARE NOT ON THE TREE r6895 (66, gating).*** *The locator's primary run is on the banked spectra and passes; the `LSTEP=2` re-run at four times the multipole sampling --- the test that the non-uniformity is not a property of the grid --- and the full-reach re-run of the nine off-path nulls both read banks that are not yet pushed, and the receipt fails on their absence rather than reporting the parts it can run as the whole. **What stands without them**: the shift's sign, its non-uniformity (which is also established statically, the superseded gate's tolerance being $0.05$ on bin-centre differences whose smallest non-zero spread is $5$), and the arms' agreement --- that last being a difference taken between two runs on one grid with one locator, which a grid property moves alike and so cannot manufacture. **What does not**: the shape of the rise with multipole, which is exactly what the finer grid is there to settle.* &#9670; ⚠ ***THE TWO BANKS LANDED AND A THIRD DEPENDENCY APPEARED WITH THEM, FOR A SUBSTANTIVE REASON r6897 (66, gating).*** *`r6893_fine_grid_*` and `r6893_full_reach_nulls_*` are on the tree and the parts reading them pass. **But the finer grid's high-multipole half is summed over wavenumber slices**, and whether that summation is valid at the second stage's own slicing and reach is a further measurement, banked separately and not yet pushed --- so the receipt still fails on one bank and the grid-independence of the rise is still not established. ⇒ *That is the code seat setting its own dependency rather than declaring the confirmation done, and the hold on the shape stands unchanged.* ⌗ **What did move**: the amplitude's own number, an envelope rescale of about a quarter rather than the third the earlier whole-spectrum move suggested, which is banked and is in the paper.* &#9670; ⛭ ***THE LOADING READING IS REFUTED BY SIGN, AND THE RESIDUAL IS LARGER THAN IT LOOKED --- r6909 (66, on the code seat's r6897+cc66.39).*** *This seat's order proposed that the contrast excess and the alternation excess were one displacement of the oscillation's zero point, and enumerated two outcomes. **The answer is a third.** Both arms' offsets approach the tight-coupling equilibrium $-R\Psi$ from below, reaching $0.78$ of it on the control and $0.76$ on the arm --- so the absolute question answers no on both and by nearly the same amount, which is the approach and not a property of either arm. ⇒ *Calibrated against a known change in loading --- the control at $\omega_b\pm8$ per cent, the estimator tracking $77$ per cent of it --- **the arm's offset falls short of what its own loading accounts for by an effective $\omega_b$ of $-2.9$ per cent.** Too much loading is what deepens troughs; this arm's monopole behaves as though it had too little.* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THE ORDER'S PREMISE WAS WRONG, WHICH IS THE PART THIS SEAT OWNS.*** *It read the alternation excess against the **sky** and the contrast against the **control** and treated them as pointing the same way. **Against the control the arm's $P_1/P_2$ is $2.141$ against $2.191$ --- lower.** The two were never aligned.* &#9670; ***SO THE TWO RESIDUALS ARE NOT ONE NUMBER, AND THE SPREAD IS NOT MARGINAL.*** *The contrast implies $+22.9$ per cent in effective loading and the alternation $-1.40$: **a factor of sixteen and opposite signs**, with the monopole offset agreeing with the alternation at $-2.94$. ⇒ *And the $+22.9$ is a **lower bound**, because the contrast's response to $\omega_b$ **saturates** --- it spans only $0.028$ across the whole range with increments falling monotonically.* ***So a four per cent contrast excess is beyond what the baryon density reaches in this construction at any value, not merely away from its fitted point.***\rcpt{P15_the_monopole_zero_point_is_not_the_channel_and_the_two_residuals_point_opposite_ways} &#9670; ⚭ ***AND THE REFRAMING, WHICH IS WORTH MORE THAN THE ELIMINATION.*** *Three channels that would each **shallow** the troughs are all larger on this arm: the monopole's offset falls short of its own loading; the dipole-to-monopole ratio at the visibility peak sits two per cent **above** the control's and rises with wavenumber, and the Doppler term fills troughs; and the visibility is **wider**, $43.6$ against $38.0$~Mpc, so this arm averages over more acoustic phase and not less --- the two widths seen side by side on the reporting path for the first time. ⇒ ***So whatever generates the contrast has to overcome all three, which means the effect to explain is bigger than the four per cent that survives them.*** &#9670; **WHAT WOULD DISCHARGE IT, RESTATED ON THAT:** *a mechanism in the **generation** of the acoustic oscillation relative to its own envelope, large enough to clear three trough-filling channels that all run the other way. ⚠ *The driving is not obviously it either*: the measured grip is $0.1717$ against the control's $0.1792$ --- weaker, and weaker driving makes less oscillation. **Four channels now point the wrong way, and that is the sharpest thing the sector has.*** &#9670; ⛑⛑ ***AND THE FOUR-FOR-FOUR DISSOLVES RATHER THAN BEING SOLVED --- r6911+cc66.40, WHICH CORRECTS THE READING DIRECTLY ABOVE.*** *This seat's order asked for the same contrast statistic at two points in the chain, and the answer is the second branch it named.* ⇒ ***The statistic reads $1.047$ on the reported spectrum and $\mathbf{0.996}$ on the source at last scattering, $0.992$ $\eta$-integrated*** --- *over four envelope windows, both envelope definitions, three term subsets and with or without the $k$-measure, the source rungs span $0.971$ to $1.005$ and the multipole rung $1.042$ to $1.052$, against a floor of $0.6$ per cent set by the statistic's own bias on a **known injected** contrast.* ⛔ ***So the excess is not in the source, and the four channels above have nothing to overcome: upstream this arm's oscillation is very slightly SHALLOWER, which is the direction all four point.*** **The effect to explain is not bigger than four per cent --- it is manufactured between wavenumber and multipole, and the hunt has been in the wrong half of the chain.** &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THIS SEAT'S NAMED ROUTE IS OUT TWICE OVER, THE FIRST HALF BEING A PREMISE CORRECTION.*** *The order's route was the arms' distances differing by six per cent, $13005$ against $13865$~Mpc. **That pair is the superseded configuration's** ($r_s=135.46/144.53$), where this arm's was the smaller; at the adjudicated minima $D_M$ is $14017.04$ here against $13954.35$ on the control --- $+0.449$ per cent and **this arm's is the LARGER**, wrong in size and in sign. The cancellation the route relies on is real --- $\ell_A$ agree to $0.085$ per cent --- so it is the six per cent and not the reasoning that fails.* ⇒ *And the route is eliminated by measurement anyway: projecting either arm's own source through the other's distance moves its contrast by under a per cent, and **removing the difference altogether takes the ratio UP**, $1.045\to1.051$.* &#9670; ⌗ ***WHERE IN THE PROJECTION, AND NO FURTHER.*** *This arm's projection retains $1.054$ times as much of its own source oscillation as the control's does; the excess **rises** with multipole where the source ratio is flat; and it is neither the visibility width acting before the kernel, nor the lensing, nor the binning, nor the two arms' different samplings of wavenumber --- `KCONT=1` puts this arm on the control's uniform grid at the same mode count with the physics untouched and the multipole rung is unchanged.* ⚠ *No mechanism is offered and none was asked for.\rcpt{P15_the_acoustic_contrast_is_not_in_the_source_and_the_projection_makes_it_without_the_distance}* &#9670; **WHAT WOULD DISCHARGE IT, RESTATED AGAIN:** *what within the projection raises this arm's contrast by five per cent when its source oscillation is if anything shallower --- or a demonstration that the projection cannot differ between two arms whose acoustic angles agree to a part in a thousand, which would make the five per cent an instrument fact rather than a physical one and is the first thing to rule out.* &#9670; ⛑⛑ ***THE CROSS TERM IS EXCLUDED AND THE EXCESS IS IN EVERY PROJECTED TERM AT ONCE --- r6915+cc66.41.*** *This seat asked which term carries it after projection and named the monopole--Doppler pair first, $j_\ell$ and $j_\ell'$ being a quarter period out of phase.* ⛔ ***And the closure gate this seat forced had to be corrected before it could be passed:*** *the **transfer** is linear in the source and the pieces do add there, but $C_\ell$ is **quadratic** in the transfer, so the projected **spectra** cannot --- the four diagonal pieces alone fall short of $D_\ell$ by up to $46$ per cent. ⌗ *That is not the third outcome this seat named: the **first** presupposes a cross term, so the two clauses were in tension and the first was the coherent one.* ⇒ **What closes is the full bilinear decomposition, ten pair spectra summing to $D_\ell$ to $1.1$ and $1.3\times10^{-15}$ relative on the two arms.** &#9670; ⛔ ***THE CROSS TERM IS NOT THE CHANNEL:*** *deleting it moves the ratio from $1.0452$ to $1.0451$, its carry running $-0.0013$ to $+0.0008$ of the $+0.045$ over four envelope windows --- **bounded at about a part in a thousand**.* &#9670; ⛑⛑ ***BECAUSE EVERY PROJECTED PIECE ALREADY CARRIES IT, WHICH IS NEITHER OUTCOME NAMED:*** *monopole $1.034$, Doppler $1.099$, polarisation $1.058$ against the full $1.045$, the ISW alone the exception at $0.989$ and worth one per cent of $D_\ell$.* ⇒ ***So it is not a term and not a pair of terms: the projection raises this arm's contrast on nearly everything it projects.*** &#9670; ***AND THE LADDER IS THE SHARPEST FORM OF IT*** --- *every term's **source** ratio is at or below $0.992$ and every term's **projected** ratio is higher: monopole $0.992\to1.034$, Doppler $0.973\to1.099$, ISW $0.972\to0.989$, polarisation $0.990\to1.058$.* &#9670; ⌗ ***THE SPLIT THIS SEAT'S REASONING WAS AFTER IS ABOUT $40/60$, THE RESPONSE THE LARGER.*** *The weight **does** differ --- the arm's monopole share $0.5225$ against $0.5092$, its Doppler $0.1753$ against $0.1840$ --- and rebuilding the arm's pieces at the control's shares gives $1.0267$, so the weights carry $+0.019$ of the $+0.045$ and each piece's own response $+0.027$; the reverse construction agrees at $+0.020$.* &#9670; ⛑ ***AND THE RETAINED FRACTION IS NOT FLAT IN $q$***, *which is the branch this seat named as the kernel-phase signature: it rises from $1.030$ in the lowest band to $1.089$ in the highest, slope $+0.0139\pm0.0021$ per unit $q$ over twelve settings and **never once negative** --- so the branch that would have killed the reading is excluded. ⌗ The intercept is $1.017\pm0.010$ and **straddles one**, so the rise is established and a constant offset is not.*\rcpt{P15_the_cross_term_is_not_the_channel_and_every_projected_term_carries_the_excess} &#9670; ⛔ ***AND r6919 (66) TAKES THE TWO CORRECTIONS cc66.41 MADE TO ITS ORDER, BOTH OF THEM THIS SEAT'S.*** *The order's closure gate said the separately projected pieces must sum to the full spectrum. **The TRANSFER is linear and the pieces add there; $C_\ell$ is QUADRATIC in the transfer, so the projected spectra cannot** --- the four diagonal pieces alone fall short of $D_\ell$ by up to $46$ per cent, gated as a measurement. And the order's own two clauses were in tension: its first outcome presupposes a cross term while its third called a failure to sum the finding. `cc66` took the coherent one and built the gate the order should have asked for. ⛔ *And the cross term --- this seat's lead hypothesis, argued from $j_\ell$ and $j_\ell'$ being a quarter period apart --- is **excluded at about a part in a thousand**.* &#9670; ⚭ ***AND THE PER-TERM LADDER IS WHAT REPLACES IT, WHICH IS A BIGGER OBJECT THAN THE HYPOTHESIS IT KILLED.*** *Every term's source ratio is at or below $0.992$ and **every term's projected ratio is higher** --- monopole $+0.042$, Doppler $+0.126$, ISW $+0.017$, polarisation $+0.067$, the whole source $+0.053$. ⇒ ***So the excess is not localised in the source at all, at any grain: the projection raises this arm's contrast on nearly everything it projects.*** ⌗ *And with the intercept straddling one while the slope never does, the projection does nothing different at the longest acoustic wavelengths and more of it at each shorter one --- which excludes an overall normalisation difference by the same fit that establishes the rise. `P15` carries that as a clause of its own, because it is what rules things out.* &#9670; ⛭ ***AND THE REFRAMING THE LADDER FORCES, WHICH IS WHERE THE ORDER GOES NEXT.*** *Write the reported spectrum as $\Delta_\ell(k)=\int S\,j_\ell(k\chi(\eta))\,\dd\eta$. **Every term decomposed so far lives in $S$, and what raises this arm's contrast is common to nearly every $S$ there is.** ⇒ *So there are exactly two things left in that integral and neither is a source term: the kernel, which is the same function on both arms, and $\chi(\eta)$ together with the visibility's weight in $\eta$ --- **the effective width the projection averages over**, which is term-independent by construction and grows with wavenumber because at fixed width there are more acoustic periods to average away at each shorter one, going to no difference at all as the window falls below one period. That last clause is the measured intercept.* ⚠ ***And the candidate is named so it can be killed rather than left implicit: this construction carries TWO RATES***, the plasma's accumulated lengths on the leaf and comoving separations read across leaves on the stacking, *so the source is accumulated on one clock while the kernel's argument is a distance read on the other* --- **a candidate and not a claim, with nothing yet checked about what the instrument integrates against what.* &#9670; ⛐ ***THE ORDER ROUTED, AND IT TAKES THE SOURCE OUT OF THE QUESTION.*** *Ⓐ feed both arms' projection machinery the **same analytic oscillating source** --- no transfer, no terms, no weights --- and measure the retained fraction against $q$: reproducing the $1.054$ and the $+0.0139$ shows the effect is the projection's geometry and **the source is irrelevant to it**, while failing to reproduce them puts it in how $S$ is distributed across the visibility and kills the paragraph above. Ⓑ then **swap the two geometric factors one at a time** --- this arm's source with the control's visibility, and this arm's source through the control's $\chi(\eta)$ --- with the standing instruction that **an ill-posed swap is to be reported as ill-posed rather than forced**, that being the shape of `r4558`'s unwired knob.* &#9670; ⛭⚭⚭ ***AND r6925 (66, on cc66.42) ENDS THE HUNT: THE MECHANISM IS THE CORPUS'S OWN RATE ASSIGNMENT.*** *A pure $g(\eta)\cos(kr_s(\eta))$ --- **no transfer, no terms, no weights**, carrying $k^{(1-n_s)/2}$ so the smooth part is exactly $\dd k/k$ on both arms --- projected through each arm's own kernel, visibility, wavenumber grid and multipole grid gives $1.066$ at $+0.0226$ per unit $q$, against the real source's $1.054$ and $+0.0139$. ⇒ ***The geometry accounts for the whole effect and OVER-delivers***, phase-independently, with a standing oscillation already carrying most of it.* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THIS SEAT'S NAMED LOCUS WAS WRONG WHILE ITS OBJECT WAS RIGHT.*** *$x_0=\eta_0-\mathrm{EE}$ on both arms, so $\chi(\eta)=\eta_0-\eta$ and $\dd\chi/\dd\eta\equiv1$ on each, with nothing touching $x_0$ on any path --- **there is nothing there to exchange**. ⇒ ***The two clocks sit between $r_s$ and $\eta$***: conformal time, and so $x_0$, is built from the stacking rate while the acoustic phase accumulates on the leaf's, and $\dd\eta_{\rm leaf}/\dd\eta_{\rm stack}$ is $1.000000$ everywhere on the control by the rate identity and runs $0.789$ to $0.913$ across $\pm3$ FWHM on the arm.* &#9670; ⚭ ***AND THE JOINT OBJECT IS THE SIGNATURE, WITH ITS THREE PROPERTIES NOW CAUSED RATHER THAN MEASURED.*** *Across the visibility FWHM the accumulated **sound horizon** agrees to $0.08$ per cent ($17.3074$ against $17.2941$~Mpc) while the **comoving distance** differs by $14.6$ ($38.042$ against $43.591$). So $\dd r_s/\dd\chi$ --- ***the sound speed the kernel sees*** --- is $0.4550$ on the control against $0.3967$ here, $12.8$ per cent lower: **term-independent by construction, growing with wavenumber, and vanishing for a window under one acoustic period**, which are exactly `cc66.41`'s three properties.* &#9670; ⌗ ***AND NEITHER SWAP CLOSES IT ALONE, WHICH WAS THE ORDER'S THIRD OUTCOME.*** *The clock swap is **ill posed as an isolation and the statistic says so rather than returning a null**: it moves the comb and the regression alternates in sign band by band, $+0.37$ to $-0.57$ --- `cc66.40`'s guard firing on the shape it was built for, ***and itself evidence that the leaf assignment is what the arm's reported peak positions need***. The width swap is well posed and **overshoots by eight**, the slope going $+0.0226$ to $+0.0931$ while the mean barely moves.*\rcpt{P15_a_source_with_no_physics_reproduces_the_contrast_and_the_two_clocks_part_company_at_the_visibility} &#9670; ⛭ ***SO THE ROW CHANGES ITS QUESTION, WHICH IS THE POINT.*** *The five per cent is no longer unexplained: **it is the instrument doing what the corpus's two-rate assignment tells it to.** ⇒ ***What is open is whether that assignment is right, not whether the transfer implements it*** --- and `P15` places the residual there in terms. ⚠ *And the rule has a gap the order now aims at: it covers separations read across leaves and scales the plasma accumulates, and **the visibility is neither** --- $\tau$ is accumulated by the plasma but differentiated per unit $\eta$, which is the stacking rate's, so nothing in the rule says which clock $g$ is a density in.* &#9670; ⛭⚭ ***AND r6929 (66, on cc66.43) ANSWERS THE RULE'S GAP: THE VISIBILITY IS NOT WHERE THE FREEDOM IS.*** *The audit found **the gap already open in the code**: every site where $\tau$ or $g$ touches a rate is on the STACKING clock --- the recombination history against $H_{\rm phys}$, the conformal grid, `ETA_LS` read off it --- while $1/k_D^{2}$ twenty lines below **is** weighted under `LEAFSCALES`. ⇒ ***So the diffusion length and the optical depth are both scales the plasma accumulates, sitting on opposite clocks, with nothing stating the choice*** --- which is what makes the other assignment admissible rather than invented, applying to $\tau$ exactly the weighting $1/k_D^{2}$ already applies to itself.* &#9670; ⌗ ***AND ON THE GEOMETRY THE TWO AGREE, STRUCTURALLY RATHER THAN LUCKILY.*** *$\dd r_s/\dd\chi$ goes $0.396733$ to $0.396957$, so $12.80$ per cent becomes $12.75$ --- a move of $0.06$ --- and the control does not move at all. **Because it is a RATIO of two accumulations across the SAME window, so re-weighting the measure re-weights both.** ⇒ *The visibility is not where the freedom is, and the $12.8$ per cent is forced by the rule as stated.* &#9670; ⚠ ***BUT THE CONTRAST AND THE COMB DISAGREE, AND NEITHER IS PICKED.*** *Read alone the other assignment moves the injection toward the real source, $1.0850\to1.0695$ and slope $+0.0242\to+0.0133$ against the real $1.0694$ and $+0.0117$ --- **but moves the arm's first peak $220\to228$ and $\ell_1/\ell_A$ $0.7290\to0.7555$, AWAY from the sky's $0.7312$**, with $P_1/P_2$ $2.142\to2.017$ and the control's comb bit-identical.
