⌗ ***ONE JUDGEMENT MADE RATHER THAN ROUTED BACK, AND IT IS ABOUT WHICH PATH OWNS WHICH NUMBER.*** *`cc66.45`'s sub-bin measurement is on the **hierarchy** path; `sec:refit-bound`'s quartet is the **line-of-sight** path's. So the sub-bin figures went into the independent-reproduction passage where that path is the subject, and the line-of-sight quartet is untouched --- **only its grid-step framing moved.** *Importing one path's located apexes into the other path's sentence would have been the same error the quartet's provenance mix-up was.** &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***AND `cc66.46` (gated r6959) ANSWERS ALL THREE PARTS, REFUTES THE ORDER'S ARGUMENT, AND CONFIRMS ITS CONCLUSION BY A DIFFERENT ROUTE --- AND IT COST NO SOLVER TIME, being analysis on banks `cc66.44` and `cc66.45` had already built.*** &#9670; ⛭ ***THE EXCESS IS SYMMETRIC ABOUT THE ENVELOPE: AN AMPLITUDE EXCESS CARRIED EQUALLY BY HEIGHTS AND DEPTHS.*** *Splitting the band variance of the envelope-normalised oscillation at its own zero --- a decomposition, the parts adding to the whole to machine precision --- gives $1.0668$ on the peak side against $1.0561$ on the trough side, and at the literal extrema the arm exceeds the control by $5.7$ per cent at the maxima against $5.3$ at the minima.* ⇒ ***So the order's caveat was right and its premise was not: $\Delta$'s trough dominance is a statement about the WHITENED RESIDUAL and does not transfer to the contrast's own decomposition.*** ⌗ *And the order's stop condition does not fire either --- the excess is not in the peaks, so the row is not pushed back onto the driving.* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THE ORDER'S CLOCK ARGUMENT IS WRONG, WITH THE STEP IT MISSED NAMED.*** *Across the `VISLEAF` family the mean trough depth moves $-2.58$ per cent against the mean peak height's $-0.77$, so **the depths ARE $3.4\times$ the more clock-responsive and that half of the reading holds.** But $\theta_D/\theta_*$ moves $-4.08$ per cent --- **more than either**.* ⇒ *** The step missed is that $r_D$ is itself Jacobian-weighted under `LEAFSCALES`, so the optical-depth re-weighting moves the diffusion length DIRECTLY: the ratio is not protected by riding one clock, because the clock re-weighting moves its numerator. *** ⇒⇒ **So the paper's discriminant of principle is the MOST sensitive of the three and not the insensitive one, and by the order's own rule --- both move together --- the depths are not a new handle on the clock.** ⌗ *Endpoints reproduce on the unit-step grid, so the response is the spectra's and not the grid's.* &#9670; ⛭⛭⛭ ***AND YET THE TROUGHS ARE WHERE IT BECOMES OBSERVABLE, FOR A REASON THAT IS THE SKY'S AND NOT THE MODEL'S: THE SKY MEASURES DEPTHS TWICE AS WELL.*** *Same data, same likelihood binning, same anchored locator, the temperature covariance propagated by Monte Carlo over two seeds and $600$ realisations each, stable to $5\times10^{-4}$. **The arm exceeds the control by the same amount in each --- $+0.01064$ in depth against $+0.01029$ in height --- while the sky's spread is $0.00588$ against $0.01135$, so the same excess reads at $+1.81\sigma$ in the depths and $+0.91$ in the heights.*** ⇒ *** AND THE CONTROL LANDS ON THE SKY'S TROUGH DEPTHS AT $-0.05\sigma$ WHILE THE ARM SITS $+1.76$ ABOVE: THE FIRST STATISTIC IN THIS SECTOR WHOSE RESIDUAL IS NOT SHARED WITH THE CONTROL *** --- against the fourth peak's $0.83\sigma$ with both models high of the sky.\rcpt{P15_the_contrast_excess_is_symmetric_about_the_envelope_and_the_troughs_are_where_the_sky_measures_it_best} ⌗ *The code seat's own framing, which is the right one: **the conclusion stands and the argument does not.*** &#9670; ⌗ ***THE GUARDS, AND ONE OF THEM RETIRES A WORRY THE ORDER CARRIED.*** *The window bias does NOT exist for this statistic --- the anchored depth is identical over the admissible windows on the sky and on both arms, **because it reads a VALUE at a located extremum rather than the LOCATION of one**, which is exactly where `cc66.45`'s parabola apex drifted six multipoles. The two statistics fail differently.* ⚠ ***But `PO-47`'s trap reproduces exactly, on depths instead of positions:*** *a free extremum search under noise returns $0.0369$ against the anchored $0.00588$, six times worse, because it latches onto noise minima. **The anchoring is necessary and not a convenience, and it is gated so the next reader cannot skip it.*** ⌗ *One real systematic, reported and not minimised: the envelope's abscissa runs the sky's depth $0.2790\to0.2830$ as the acoustic scale that sets it goes $298\to305$, about a third of a sigma.* &#9670; ⌗ ***AND A CANDIDATE OFFERED UNASKED, CARRIED HERE AS A RUN AND NOT A RESULT:*** *the amplitude excess is symmetric, grows with wavenumber, and survives every localisation attempt --- and the one thing which multiplies an oscillation symmetrically about its envelope without moving its phase is **the transfer's own normalisation across the visibility**, not a phase and not a scale. The handle named is the source's conformal-time dependence across the window, which `cc66.42`'s injection replaced and `cc66.43` named as the un-measured piece.* ⛔ ***BOUND, CARRIED: $1.8\sigma$ is not a detection.*** *It is $1.8$ of the sky's own spread on ONE statistic, and the same differencing at the fourth peak gave $0.83$.* &#9670; **WHAT WOULD DISCHARGE IT, UNCHANGED IN SUBSTANCE AND SHARPER IN FORM:** *whether the two-rate assignment is right.* ⌗ *What `cc66.43` adds is that the rule as stated **does not determine** the visibility's clock, that the instrument's two plasma-accumulated objects are already on opposite clocks, and that the peak positions support the choice it currently makes.* ⌗ *And what `cc66.44` adds is the arbiter's own resolution --- the comb pins the clock only to $\pm0.24$ of the family --- and that **the sky's value is not in the family at all**, so discharging this row cannot come from choosing between the two clocks: neither reaches the first peak the sky has.* ⌗ *And what `cc66.45` adds is that the fourth peak --- the sharpest external-referent residual this sector had --- is four fifths the control's, that what remains is one near-constant multipole inside the sky's own spread, and that the instrument locating it is good to three hundredths: **so discharging this row cannot come from the comb's residual either.*** ⌗ *And `cc66.46` leaves the row with one live handle rather than none: the excess is an amplitude excess symmetric about the envelope, the clock cannot be read from the trough depths any better than from $\theta_D/\theta_*$, and **what the sky can actually see is the depths --- $1.8\sigma$ of its own spread, and the only part of this sector's residual the control does not reproduce.*** ⌗ ⛭⛭ *And `cc66.47` gives the row the first channel it has that carries a **measured** share rather than none: the transfer's own normalisation across the visibility window, whose $\eta$-profile is now measured on both arms as a function. Through the instrument's own two-coefficient response the arm's window narrowing delivers **$23$ to $45$ per cent of the band contrast excess above $q=1.9$**, and imposing the arm's spread on the control closes $20$ to $39$ per cent of it --- the same answer by a different road, the narrowing for all of it being a factor $2.7$ above what the arm has.* ⇒ ⛔ *But it cannot be the whole of it, and the reason comes from `cc66.46` rather than from any size estimate: **the excess is symmetric about the envelope and this channel is peak-weighted by a factor of five and a half** --- $81$ per cent of the peak-height excess against $14$ per cent of the trough-depth excess. ⌗ *And the window's phase spread is nearly equal on the two arms for a reason worth recording on the row: the arm's visibility is $14.6$ per cent wider in conformal time while its phase accumulates $13.7$ per cent slower per unit of it, so **the two-rate structure reaches the window and then cancels inside it to one part in ninety.*** ⌗ *What remains un-tested and is named as such is the term mix --- the arm holding more of its window's power in the monopole in every band --- which is the one part of a normalisation difference that is not a smearing and so need not vanish at long wavelength, where the excess's own $q$-independent offset of $1.040$ sits.* | P15 `sec:refit-bound`; P15 `sec:scope`; P15 `sec:tensions` |
| **PO-23** | **THE ULTRAVIOLET DEFINITION OF THE MODE SUMS.** &#9670; ***AND THE `r6445` CLAUSE IS RESOLVED r6453 (64) --- STRUCTURALLY, AND IT DOES NOT CLOSE THE ROW.*** *That clause asked whether coupling acts on this sum by deforming the spectrum. **It cannot be established ahead of this row, because it asks what the coupling does to THE SUM and the open item IS the definition of that sum.** ⇒ ***So `60`'s table is exactly the reach of the free-spectrum method, and the remainder is not reachable by refining it*** --- the conditional is the boundary of the technique, not a weakness in the work.* &#9670; ⛭ ***AND THIS ROW IS THE LAST OF A NAMED TRIPLE, TWO OF WHICH ARE NOW SAID.*** *`P07`: what remains open is the definition of the interacting tower --- **the spectrum of $\hat\Gamma$, whether it is bounded below, and the ultraviolet definition of the mode sums** --- and then, in its own voice, "**two of those three can now be said**": the operator **is** bounded below, and its spectrum **is** computable branch by branch, $\tfrac14$ under normal ordering and $\tfrac34$ under symmetric with one mode occupied. **Two of the three walls are built.*** &#9670; *And `P10`'s coupling is not what a deformation table deforms: $\hat\Gamma$ acts on the **scale-factor** sector's inverse-square coefficient, a direct integral over the graviton momenta, with the $\mu_n$ untouched --- and the boundary condition is **settled**, thermal regularity supplying it fibre by fibre since $\kappa$ belongs to the background horizon. `P10`'s own note: "**do not report the coupled sector as a residual quantization freedom**".* &#9670; ⛔ ***AND THE RE-SCOPING THIS INVITES IS ONE THE CORPUS HAS ALREADY MADE AND REVERSED.*** *`P07` frames the item as whether the coupled sector is a consistent well-defined theory, which reads like any interacting theory's problem, and the obvious move is to call the remainder **not CR-specific** as `PO-36` was at `r6407`. **The ledger records that move: "RESTORED `r1291` --- wrongly kill-listed `r1243` as 'not a CR open' and propagated as such this session; a genuine open, not a false-open.
